巴西亚马逊地区急性发热性疾病哨兵 监测:实验室结果和流行病学意义

Sentinel surveillance of acute febrile illness in a Brazilian Amazon region: laboratory findings and epidemiological implications
作者： Barbosa E, da Costa A, Ferreira N, Paião H, Freire W, de Paula A, Honorato L, Morais V, Rego M, Ribeiro E
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在描述 2018-2020 年阿马帕州报告的急性发热性疾病 (AFI) 的病因学特征,重点关注虫媒病毒循环、细菌贡献和新出现的病原体。185 人(25.4%)获得确诊,其中 209 例呈阳性,其中包括 23 例合并感染病例。加强 监测、扩大实验室能力和优先考虑鉴别诊断对于掌握流行病学动态和指导公共卫生干预措施至关重要。关键词:登革热、钩端螺旋体病、虫媒病毒感染、基孔肯雅热、细小病毒 B19 材料和方法研究设计和人群使用方便样本进行了观察性、横断面、回顾性和描述性研究。对 2018 年(n = 20)、2019 年(n = 581)和 2020 年(n = 126)收集的 727 名个体的样本进行了分析。本研究旨在寻找巴西阿马帕州亚马逊地区 2018 年至 2022 年报告的 AFI 病例中病毒和其他感染的潜在病因。由主治医生酌情进行其他检测,例如 血清学 和聚合酶链反应 (PCR),结果记录在 GAL 上。摘要巴西阿马帕州位于亚马逊地区,与法属圭亚那接壤,是传染病 监测 的战略哨兵地点。森林砍伐、手工金矿开采和人口流动性大等生态压力导致了虫媒病毒、细菌感染和其他病原体的复杂 传播 动态。对727例AFI病例进行回顾性分析。实验室诊断是通过分子和血清学检测以及对不同病原体的系统检测来建立的。登革病毒 是主要病因...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在确定2018年至2022年巴西阿马帕州亚马逊地区报告的急性发热性疾病(AFI)病例中病毒和其他感染的潜在病因,以更好地了解该地区传染病的流行病学状况。
设计与对象
该研究是2018年至2022年在巴西阿马帕州亚马逊地区公共卫生实验室进行的前哨监测研究。共评估了727人,确诊185例(25.4%),其中23例合并感染。
核心方法
诊断测试包括免疫球蛋白 M (IgM) 和 NS1 的酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA)、电化学发光、逆转录 PCR (RT-PCR) 和测序。样本储存在-20°C,并运送到圣保罗热带医学研究所(LV-IMT-FMUSP)病毒学实验室进行进一步测试,在那里进行实时PCR检测以鉴定病毒因子。
主要结果
登革病毒 是最常见的 诊断,在 650 次测试中占 109 例(16.8%)。其他值得注意的发现包括钩端螺旋体病(20/98;20.4%)、基孔肯雅热(16/86;18.6%)和麻疹(15/53;28.3%)。非登革热阳性病例包括寨卡病毒(9/62;14.5%)、细小病毒B19(6/21;28.6%)和细菌感染(5/17;29.4%)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了 登革病毒 在该地区 AFI 病因学中的重要作用,并观察到了显着的合并感染。这项研究为亚马逊地区登革热和其他病原体的分布和 患病率 提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为亚马逊地区登革热和其他共感染病原体的针对性 监测 和诊断策略提供信息,有助于制定更有效的公共卫生干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究的描述性分析可能会限制解释的深度,并且多种诊断方法的使用可能会引入变异性。由于研究的观察性质和潜在的混杂因素,证据的强度适中。

Sentinel surveillance of acute febrile illness in a Brazilian Amazon region: laboratory findings and epidemiological implications

Authors: Barbosa E, da Costa A, Ferreira N, Paião H, Freire W, de Paula A, Honorato L, Morais V, Rego M, Ribeiro E
Original abstract (English)
This study aims to characterize the etiologies of acute febrile illness (AFI) reported in Amapa State in 2018-2020, focusing on arbovirus circulation, bacterial contributions, and emerging pathogens. Definitive diagnoses were obtained in 185 individuals (25.4%), yielding 209 positive results and including 23 cases of co-infection. Strengthening surveillance, expanding laboratory capacity, and prioritizing differential diagnostics are essential to capture epidemiological dynamics and guide public health interventions.KEYWORDS: Dengue, Leptospirosis, Arbovirus infections, Chikungunya fever, Parvovirus B19 MATERIAL AND METHODSStudy design and populationAn observational, cross-sectional, retrospective, and descriptive study was conducted using a convenience sample. Samples from 727 individuals, collected in 2018 (n = 20), 2019 (n = 581), and 2020 (n = 126), were analyzed.This study aims to find the potential etiologies of viral and other infections in AFI cases reported from 2018 to 2022 in the Amazon region of Amapa State, Brazil. Additional tests, such as serology and polymerase chain reaction (PCR) for other pathogens, were performed at the discretion of attending physicians, with results recorded on GAL. ABSTRACTThe Amapa State, Brazil, in the Amazon region and bordering French Guiana, is a strategic sentinel location for infectious disease surveillance. Ecological pressures such as deforestation, artisanal gold mining, and high human mobility contribute to complex transmission dynamics of arboviruses, bacterial infections, and other pathogens. A retrospective analysis was carried out with 727 AFI cases. Laboratory diagnoses were established using molecular and serological assays, with systematic testing for different pathogens. Dengue virus was the predominant etiolog...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to identify the potential etiologies of viral and other infections in acute febrile illness (AFI) cases reported from 2018 to 2022 in the Amazon region of Amapa State, Brazil, to better understand the epidemiological landscape of infectious diseases in the area.
Design and population
The study was a sentinel surveillance conducted in public health laboratories in the Amazon region of Amapa State, Brazil, from 2018 to 2022. A total of 727 individuals were evaluated, with definitive diagnoses established in 185 cases (25.4%), including 23 cases of co-infection.
Core methods
Diagnostic tests included enzyme linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) for immunoglobulin M (IgM) and NS1, electrochemiluminescence, reverse transcription PCR (RT-PCR), and sequencing. Samples were stored at −20 °C and shipped to the Virology Laboratory of the Institute of Tropical Medicine of Sao Paulo (LV-IMT-FMUSP) for further testing, where real-time PCR assays were performed to identify viral agents.
Main results
Dengue virus was the most frequent diagnosis, accounting for 109 cases (16.8%) out of 650 tests. Other notable findings included leptospirosis (20/98; 20.4%), chikungunya (16/86; 18.6%), and measles (15/53; 28.3%). Non-dengue positive cases included Zika (9/62; 14.5%), parvovirus B19 (6/21; 28.6%), and bacterial infections (5/17; 29.4%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as highlighting the significant role of dengue virus in the etiology of AFI in the region, with notable co-infections observed. This study provides novel insights into the distribution and prevalence of dengue and other pathogens in the Amazon region.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance and diagnostic strategies for dengue and other co-infecting pathogens in the Amazon region, aiding in the development of more effective public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's descriptive analysis may limit the depth of interpretation, and the use of multiple diagnostic methods could introduce variability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and the potential for confounding factors.

瓦萨卡拉预防登革热:了解、态度和实践,约普贡东部,科特迪瓦,2025 年

Prévention de la dengue à Wassakara : enquête sur les connaissances, attitudes et pratiques, Yopougon-Est, Côte d’Ivoire, 2025
作者： Ama Kounangui Marie Noelle Ano, Daniel Kouadio Ekra, Maky Diallo, Aristide Goueu Doua, Isaac Tiembré Issaka
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是一种传染病,在西非传播,并在科特迪瓦传播。卫生机构的努力,以及阿比让城市和周边地区的疾病诊断和控制困难。
设计与对象
该研究重点是瓦萨卡拉,阿比让约普贡社区的一个偏爱地区,旨在评估有关预防登革热的成年人的知识、态度和实践 (CAP)。共有 316 个成人选项可供选择。
核心方法
联合国调查问卷结构允许社会人口统计和认识、态度和实践 (CAP) 的恢复。统计分析包括双因素测试和影响因素识别的逻辑回归。
主要结果
大多数人 (94,3 %) 认为登革热存在,对 传播 的特殊认识、症状和预防不足:7,6 % 获得满意分数,并接受 2,8 % 儿童保护实践法规。健康的动员活动是可行的。
解释与创新
该研究总结了加强卫生教育行动、社区代理人的形成和早期适应措施的必要性。多部门战略和参与性是增强社区韧性不可或缺的。
科研与公卫意义
卫生教育和社区动员对于预防登革热的重要性至关重要。我们提供有关针对当地情况进行干预措施的信息。
局限与证据强度
La méthodologie repose sur des données recuillies via un Questionnaire structuré,ce qui limite la profondeur des analysis.结果是横向关系的基础,它会影响结论的概括。

Prévention de la dengue à Wassakara : enquête sur les connaissances, attitudes et pratiques, Yopougon-Est, Côte d’Ivoire, 2025

Authors: Ama Kounangui Marie Noelle Ano, Daniel Kouadio Ekra, Maky Diallo, Aristide Goueu Doua, Isaac Tiembré Issaka
Original abstract (English)
Introduction : La dengue est une maladie infectieuse transmise par des moustiques et qui connaît une forte croissance en Afrique de l’Ouest, notamment en Côte d’Ivoire. Malgré les efforts des institutions sanitaires, la maladie reste sous-diagnostiquée et difficile à contrôler, particulièrement dans les zones urbaines et périurbaines comme Abidjan. Cette étude s’est focalisée sur Wassakara, un quartier défavorisé de la commune de Yopougon à Abidjan, afin d’évaluer les connaissances, attitudes et pratiques (CAP) des adultes concernant la prévention contre la dengue. Méthode : Une enquête transversale a été menée auprès de 316 adultes sélectionnés aléatoirement. Un questionnaire structuré a permis de recueillir des données sociodémographiques et le niveau de CAP. Les analyses statistiques ont inclus des tests du khi-deux et des régressions logistiques pour identifier les facteurs influents. Résultats : Si la majorité (94,3 %) connaît l’existence de la dengue, les connaissances spécifiques sur sa transmission, ses symptômes et la prévention sont insuffisantes : seuls 7,6 % ont obtenu un score de connaissances satisfaisant, et à peine 2,8 % adoptaient régulièrement des pratiques protectrices. La mobilisation lors des campagnes de salubrité était faible. Les facteurs associés à de meilleurs scores sont l’âge, le niveau d’éducation, la taille du ménage et l’engagement collectif. Conclusion : L’étude conclut à la nécessité d’intensifier les actions ciblées d’éducation sanitaire, la formation des agents communautaires et la fourniture de moyens adaptés. Une stratégie multisectorielle et participative est indispensable pour renforcer la résilience communautaire.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
La dengue est une maladie infectieuse transmise par des moustiques et qui connaît une forte croissance en Afrique de l’Ouest, notamment en Côte d’Ivoire. Malgré les efforts des institutions sanitaires, la maladie reste sous-diagnostiquée et difficile à contrôler, particulièrement dans les zones urbaines et périurbaines comme Abidjan.
Design and population
Cette étude s’est focalisée sur Wassakara, un quartier défavorisé de la commune de Yopougon à Abidjan, afin d’évaluer les connaissances, attitudes et pratiques (CAP) des adultes concernant la prévention contre la dengue. Une enquête transversale a été menée auprès de 316 adultes sélectionnés aléatoirement.
Core methods
Un questionnaire structuré a permis de recueillir des données sociodémographiques et le niveau de connaissances, attitudes et pratiques (CAP). Les analyses statistiques ont inclus des tests du khi-deux et des régressions logistiques pour identifier les facteurs influents.
Main results
Si la majorité (94,3 %) connaît l’existence de la dengue, les connaissances spécifiques sur sa transmission, ses symptômes et la prévention sont insuffisantes : seuls 7,6 % ont obtenu un score de connaissances satisfaisant, et à peine 2,8 % adoptaient régulièrement des pratiques protectrices. La mobilisation lors des campagnes de salubrité était faible.
Interpretation and novelty
L’étude conclut à la nécessité d’intensifier les actions ciblées d’éducation sanitaire, la formation des agents communautaires et la fourniture de moyens adaptés. Une stratégie multisectorielle et participative est indispensable pour renforcer la résilience communautaire.
Scientific and public-health significance
Les résultats soulignent l’importance d’une éducation sanitaire ciblée et d’une mobilisation communautaire pour améliorer la prévention de la dengue. Ils offrent des informations précieuses pour concevoir des interventions adaptées aux contextes locaux.
Limitations and evidence strength
La méthodologie repose sur des données recueillies via un questionnaire structuré, ce qui limite la profondeur des analyses. Les résultats sont basés sur une enquête transversale, ce qui peut affecter la généralisation des conclusions.

虫媒病毒诊断:从传统方法到新兴技术——以基孔肯雅病毒为中心的综述

Arbovirus diagnostics: from conventional methods to emerging technologies—a chikungunya virus-centric review
作者： Yongqing Zhu, Jiaxuan Li, Haiyan Mao, Keda Chen
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒仍然是一个重要的全球健康问题,因为它们的分布不断扩大、传播 模式多样化,以及与准确和及时的 诊断 相关的挑战。本综述以基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)为代表模型,重点关注不同感染阶段的诊断策略以及影响共循环环境中 CHIKV 检测的关键诊断陷阱。在这篇综述中,我们总结了当前基于实验室的 CHIKV 检测和差异 诊断 方法,包括病毒分离、抗原检测、核酸扩增和血清学测定,并讨论了它们的应用、优点和局限性。我们进一步回顾了新兴诊断技术的最新发展,包括下一代测序、基于 CRISPR 的检测方法、基于适体的识别系统、生物传感器和即时检测平台,强调了它们的潜在优势和当前局限性。特别讨论了涉及 登革病毒 (DENV) 和寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 的比较示例,以说明与 CHIKV 诊断 相关的交叉反应和错误分类风险。特别关注影响诊断准确性和实际实施的持续存在的问题,包括共循环虫媒病毒之间的血清学交叉反应性,以及将先进技术转化为适用于现场和资源有限环境的可用工具的挑战。总的来说,这篇综述总结了 CHIKV 相关诊断的进展,并概述了开发更可靠、快速和广泛适用的检测策略的未来机会。

问题与背景
虫媒病毒仍然是一个重要的全球健康问题,因为它们的分布不断扩大、传播 模式多样化,以及与准确和及时的 诊断 相关的挑战。

主要结果
特别讨论了涉及 登革病毒 (DENV) 和寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 的比较示例,以说明与 CHIKV 诊断 相关的交叉反应和错误分类风险。

总的来说,这篇综述总结了 CHIKV 相关诊断的进展,并概述了开发更可靠、快速和广泛适用的检测策略的未来机会。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性或证据强度。

Arbovirus diagnostics: from conventional methods to emerging technologies—a chikungunya virus-centric review

Authors: Yongqing Zhu, Jiaxuan Li, Haiyan Mao, Keda Chen
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses remain an important global health concern because of their expanding distribution, diverse transmission patterns, and the challenges associated with accurate and timely diagnosis. This review is centered on Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) as a representative model, focusing on diagnostic strategies across different stages of infection and the key diagnostic pitfalls that affect CHIKV detection in co-circulation settings. In this review, we summarize current laboratory-based approaches for CHIKV detection and differential diagnosis, including virus isolation, antigen detection, nucleic acid amplification and serological assays, with discussion of their applications, strengths and limitations. We further review recent developments in emerging diagnostic technologies, including next-generation sequencing, CRISPR-based detection methods, aptamer-based recognition systems, biosensors, and point-of-care testing platforms, highlighting their potential advantages and current limitations. Comparative examples involving dengue virus (DENV) and Zika virus (ZIKV) are discussed specifically to illustrate cross-reactivity and misclassification risks relevant to CHIKV diagnosis. Particular attention is given to persistent issues affecting diagnostic accuracy and practical implementation, including serological cross-reactivity among co-circulating arboviruses and the challenges of translating advanced technologies into accessible tools for field and resource-limited settings. Overall, this review summarizes progress in CHIKV-relevant diagnostics and outlines future opportunities for developing more reliable, rapid, and widely applicable detection strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Arboviruses remain an important global health concern because of their expanding distribution, diverse transmission patterns, and the challenges associated with accurate and timely diagnosis.
Design and population
This review is centered on Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) as a representative model, focusing on diagnostic strategies across different stages of infection and the key diagnostic pitfalls that affect CHIKV detection in co-circulation settings.
Core methods
We further review recent developments in emerging diagnostic technologies, including next-generation sequencing, CRISPR-based detection methods, aptamer-based recognition systems, biosensors, and point-of-care testing platforms, highlighting their potential advantages and current limitations.
Main results
Comparative examples involving dengue virus (DENV) and Zika virus (ZIKV) are discussed specifically to illustrate cross-reactivity and misclassification risks relevant to CHIKV diagnosis.
Interpretation and novelty
Particular attention is given to persistent issues affecting diagnostic accuracy and practical implementation, including serological cross-reactivity among co-circulating arboviruses and the challenges of translating advanced technologies into accessible tools for field and resource-limited settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
Overall, this review summarizes progress in CHIKV-relevant diagnostics and outlines future opportunities for developing more reliable, rapid, and widely applicable detection strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength for the study.

巴西虫媒病毒传播:范围审查协议

Arbovirus circulation in Brazil: a scoping review protocol
作者： Romário de Sousa Oliveira, Valéria Cristina Soares Pinheiro
摘要中文翻译由伊蚊物种(主要是埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊)传播的虫媒病毒是巴西的一项重大公共卫生挑战。近几十年来,登革病毒 (DENV)、寨卡病毒 (ZIKV)、基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 的共同传播、重新出现和反复流行,以及黄热病病毒 (YFV) 潜在的再城市化风险,给不同地理区域带来了严重的健康、社会和经济负担。尽管进行了广泛的流行病学 监测 和研究,但关于感染频率、人群易感性、诊断方法和区域疾病负担的局部差异的文献仍然支离破碎。对已发表的证据进行系统绘制——特别关注实验室检测方法(分子、血清学或病毒分离)证明的人类感染和暴露——对于综合当前知识、描绘时空模式并确定巴西城市和城郊环境中伊蚊传播虫媒病毒研究的关键证据差距是必要的。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决对巴西伊蚊(尤其是 登革病毒)传播的虫媒病毒相关的感染频率、人群易感性、诊断方法和区域疾病负担的当地差异的零碎理解。这项研究的动机是这些病毒带来的重大公共卫生挑战,这些病毒给不同地理区域造成了严重的健康、社会和经济负担。
设计与对象
该范围审查协议概述了已发表证据的系统图谱,重点关注人类感染和接触 登革病毒 和其他伊蚊传播的虫媒病毒,特别是在巴西城市和城郊环境中。该研究跨越近几十年来,包括 登革病毒、寨卡病毒、基孔肯雅病毒的共同传播、重新出现和反复流行以及黄热病病毒潜在的再城市化风险的数据。
核心方法
该审查将系统地绘制已发表的证据,特别关注通过分子、血清学或病毒分离技术等​​实验室检测方法证明的人类感染和暴露。该研究将综合现有知识,描绘时空模式,并确定巴西城市和城郊环境中伊蚊传播的虫媒病毒研究中的关键证据差距。
主要结果
该研究报告称,关于巴西 登革病毒 和其他伊蚊传播的虫媒病毒相关的感染频率、人群易感性、诊断方法和区域疾病负担的当地差异,文献仍然支离破碎。它强调需要全面综合当前的知识来弥补这些差距。
解释与创新
作者解释说,零碎的文献需要进行系统绘图,以更好地了解巴西 登革病毒 和其他伊蚊传播的虫媒病毒的空间和时间动态。这种方法的新颖之处在于它侧重于综合当前知识并确定关键证据差距。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为有针对性的 监测、改进的诊断策略和更有效的公共卫生干预措施提供信息,以减轻巴西 监测 和其他伊蚊传播的虫媒病毒的影响。他们还强调了解决疾病负担和感染模式的区域差异的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于范围审查方案,不提供原始结果。证据仅是抽象的,由于缺乏详细数据和审查的探索性,研究结果的可信度较低。

Arbovirus circulation in Brazil: a scoping review protocol

Authors: Romário de Sousa Oliveira, Valéria Cristina Soares Pinheiro
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses transmitted by Aedes species—predominantly Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus—represent a major public health challenge in Brazil. The co-circulation, re-emergence, and recurring epidemics of Dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), alongside the potential re-urbanization risk of Yellow Fever virus (YFV) over recent decades, have imposed severe health, social, and economic burdens across diverse geographic regions. Despite extensive epidemiological surveillance and research, literature remains fragmented regarding local variations in infection frequency, population susceptibility, diagnostic approaches, and regional disease burdens. A systematic mapping of published evidence—specifically focusing on human infection and exposure evidenced by laboratory detection methods (molecular, serological, or viral isolation)—is necessary to synthesize current knowledge, delineate spatial-temporal patterns, and identify critical evidence gaps in Aedes-borne arboviral research across Brazilian urban and peri-urban environments.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the fragmented understanding of local variations in infection frequency, population susceptibility, diagnostic approaches, and regional disease burdens associated with arboviruses transmitted by Aedes species, particularly Dengue virus, in Brazil. The research was motivated by the significant public health challenge posed by these viruses, which have caused severe health, social, and economic burdens across diverse geographic regions.
Design and population
This scoping review protocol outlines a systematic mapping of published evidence focusing on human infection and exposure to Dengue virus and other Aedes-borne arboviruses, specifically in Brazilian urban and peri-urban environments. The study spans recent decades and includes data on the co-circulation, re-emergence, and recurring epidemics of Dengue virus, Zika virus, Chikungunya virus, and the potential re-urbanization risk of Yellow Fever virus.
Core methods
The review will systematically map published evidence, specifically focusing on human infection and exposure evidenced by laboratory detection methods such as molecular, serological, or viral isolation techniques. The study will synthesize current knowledge, delineate spatial-temporal patterns, and identify critical evidence gaps in Aedes-borne arboviral research across Brazilian urban and peri-urban environments.
Main results
The study reports that literature remains fragmented regarding local variations in infection frequency, population susceptibility, diagnostic approaches, and regional disease burdens related to Dengue virus and other Aedes-borne arboviruses in Brazil. It highlights the need for a comprehensive synthesis of current knowledge to address these gaps.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the fragmented literature necessitates a systematic mapping to better understand the spatial and temporal dynamics of Dengue virus and other Aedes-borne arboviruses in Brazil. This approach is novel in its focus on synthesizing current knowledge and identifying critical evidence gaps.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance, improved diagnostic strategies, and more effective public health interventions to mitigate the impact of Dengue virus and other Aedes-borne arboviruses in Brazil. They also highlight the importance of addressing regional variations in disease burden and infection patterns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a scoping review protocol and does not present original results. The evidence is abstract-only, and the confidence in the findings is moderate due to the lack of detailed data and the exploratory nature of the review.

登革热流行病学和社会人口学视角:米纳斯吉拉斯州的现状分析

A dengue sob uma perspectiva epidemiológica e sociodemográfica: uma análise sobre o estado de Minas Gerais
作者： Maria Cristina Vasconcelos Teixeira
摘要中文翻译巴西登革热流行病学背景是当地和流行病期间的变化,代表了公众的持续发展。我们对米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热流行病学和社会人口学的总体分析进行了客观分析,并与乌贝兰迪亚的市政部门、最近的科学文献证据的一部分进行了研究。本书是有关研究的文献、描述和探索,是在国家和国际基础上实现的。我们的结果是,在过去的一段时间内,我们与社会环境、地区因素以及对萨乌德系统产生了重大影响。结论是米纳斯吉拉斯州长期存在的登革热问题,是流行病学、控制媒介和相互间交流的综合策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了巴西登革热面临的持续挑战,其特点是流行期和流行病 暴发 之间的振荡,旨在分析米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热的流行病学和社会人口学概况,重点是乌贝兰迪亚。
设计与对象
这是一项描述性和探索性的书目研究,使用国家和国际数据库进行,以分析米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热的流行病学和社会人口学概况,重点关注乌贝兰迪亚。
核心方法
该研究采用描述性和探索性的文献研究方法,利用国家和国际数据库收集和分析有关登革热的最新科学证据。
主要结果
结果表明,在一年中的某些时段,登革热的 发病率 较高,与社会环境因素、区域不平等密切相关,并对统一卫生系统产生重大影响。
解释与创新
作者解释说,登革热仍然是米纳斯吉拉斯州的一个重大公共卫生问题,需要采取流行病学 监测、媒介 控制和跨部门行动的综合策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要采取综合的 监测 和控制策略,强调解决社会环境因素和区域不平等以减轻登革热对卫生系统影响的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对书目研究的依赖而受到限制,书目研究可能无法捕获所有当前或本地数据;然而,最近科学证据的使用为研究结果提供了适度的可信度。

A dengue sob uma perspectiva epidemiológica e sociodemográfica: uma análise sobre o estado de Minas Gerais

Authors: Maria Cristina Vasconcelos Teixeira
Original abstract (English)
O contexto epidemiológico da dengue no Brasil caracteriza-se por oscilações entre períodos endêmicos e surtos epidêmicos, representando um desafio contínuo à saúde pública. Este estudo tem como objetivo geral analisar o perfil epidemiológico e sociodemográfico da dengue no estado de Minas Gerais, com ênfase no município de Uberlândia, a partir de evidências da literatura científica recente. Trata-se de uma pesquisa bibliográfica, de caráter descritivo e exploratório, realizada em bases de dados nacionais e internacionais. Os resultados indicam elevada incidência da doença em determinados períodos do ano, forte associação com fatores socioambientais, desigualdades regionais e impacto significativo sobre o Sistema Único de Saúde. Conclui-se que a dengue permanece como um importante problema de saúde pública em Minas Gerais, exigindo estratégias integradas de vigilância epidemiológica, controle vetorial e ações intersetoriais.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the continuous challenge of dengue in Brazil, characterized by oscillations between endemic periods and epidemic outbreaks, aiming to analyze the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of dengue in Minas Gerais, with emphasis on Uberlândia.
Design and population
This is a descriptive and exploratory bibliographic research conducted using national and international databases to analyze the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of dengue in Minas Gerais, focusing on Uberlândia.
Core methods
The study employed a descriptive and exploratory bibliographic research approach, utilizing national and international databases to gather and analyze recent scientific evidence on dengue.
Main results
The results indicate a high incidence of dengue during certain periods of the year, a strong association with socio-environmental factors, regional inequalities, and a significant impact on the Unified Health System.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that dengue remains a significant public health problem in Minas Gerais, requiring integrated strategies for epidemiological surveillance, vector control, and intersectoral actions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for integrated surveillance and control strategies, highlighting the importance of addressing socio-environmental factors and regional inequalities to mitigate dengue's impact on the health system.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on bibliographic research, which may not capture all current or local data; however, the use of recent scientific evidence provides moderate confidence in the findings.

启动和促进以患者为导向的虫媒病毒研究的研究者渠道

Priming and boosting the investigator pipeline for patient-oriented arboviral research
作者： Matthew H. Collins
摘要中文翻译该项目以 Matthew Collins 博士作为传染病和旅行医学转化虫媒病毒学研究和临床工作领导者持续不断发展的职业生涯为基础,这里提出的工作源于一项正在进行的临床试验,旨在更好地了解 登革病毒 的保护性免疫。作为埃默里大学医学院传染病系的副教授,自乔治亚医学院血管生物学中心(一家负责改善乔治亚州患者健康结果的公共机构)本科学习起,他就一直参与以患者为导向的研究 (POR)。最终,柯林斯出于通过严格的发现科学来改善患者治疗效果的兴趣和动机,攻读了医学博士、博士学位,他对热带和新出现的疾病,特别是 媒介 传播的疾病产生了浓厚的兴趣,这些疾病代表了一个可解决的全球健康问题,给人类带来了巨大的疾病负担。该项目将重点关注一组密切相关的黄病毒病毒(包括登革热、寨卡病毒、黄热病和西尼罗河病毒),同时解决许多病毒的基本知识空白:不同暴露的组合是否会对特定病毒产生更好的免疫力?利用一套独特的人体样本、工具和试剂,我们将检查连续 疫苗接种 与不同登革热 疫苗 产生的黄病毒免疫的广度,并评估登革热感染如何影响黄热病保护性免疫的强度和持久性。这项研究产生的数据将对正在进行的产品开发以及预防黄病毒感染的现有方法的公共卫生政策产生直接影响。为了实现该项目的科学目标和他更大的职业目标,柯林斯博士制定了全面的职业发展计划,其中包括与顾问、导师和合作者团队的联系以及一系列重点知识(疫苗学)、技能(生物统计学和试验设计)和领导力(团队科学、学术职业发展和指导)建设和培训活动。这些努力主要是通过埃默里大学的现有资源完成的,包括临床研究训练营(埃默里)、疫苗学研讨会(Keystone)、ADVAC(疫苗学高级课程(日内瓦大学))和 COMPASS(埃默里医学院职业优化和学术成功与支持辅导计划)。最后,该提案为招募和指导 POR 学员创造了丰富的机会。柯林斯对 POR 的承诺与 NIH 的良好记录以及基金会的资助和指导相匹配。他的学员包括本科生、硕士生和博士生、住院医师和研究员以及博士后。该提案增加了 Collins 博士培养下一代 POR 研究人员的能力和努力。埃默里大学的环境为大量才华横溢的学员提供了机会,他们将受益于团队科学、跨学科虫媒病毒学的机会,以及通过项目进行的实验设计、高级分析和科学传播方面的强有力的培训,这些项目对改善人类健康和减少虫媒病毒疾病的持续努力做出了重大贡献。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决理解不同暴露组合是否可以增强对 登革病毒 免疫力的根本差距。
设计与对象
该研究是一项正在进行的临床试验,旨在更好地了解对 登革病毒 的保护性免疫力,涉及人体样本,并重点关注不同登革热 疫苗 的连续 疫苗接种。
核心方法
这些方法包括利用一组独特的人类样本、工具和试剂来检查连续 疫苗接种 产生的黄病毒免疫的广度,并评估登革热感染对黄热病免疫的影响。
主要结果
该研究直接观察了连续疫苗接种与不同登革热疫苗产生的黄病毒免疫的广度,并评估登革热感染如何影响黄热病保护性免疫的强度和持久性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,这些发现将有助于了解 免疫应答 对登革热和相关黄病毒的影响,为联合暴露的潜在益处提供新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为正在进行的预防黄病毒感染(特别是登革热和黄热病)的产品开发和公共卫生政策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于正在进行的临床试验,该试验可能在普遍性和控制方面存在局限性,但该研究的设计和对 登革病毒 的关注为其结论提供了中等程度的可信度。

Priming and boosting the investigator pipeline for patient-oriented arboviral research

Authors: Matthew H. Collins
Original abstract (English)
This project is anchored in Dr. Matthew Collins’ sustained and growing career as a leader in translational arbovirology research and clinical work in Infectious Diseases and Travel Medicine, with the work proposed here growing out of an ongoing clinical trial designed to better understand protective immunity to dengue virus. As an Associate Professor in the Division of Infectious Diseases in the Emory University School of Medicine, he has been involved in patient-oriented research (POR) since his undergraduate experiences in the Vascular Biology Center at the Medical College of Georgia, a public institution charged with improving health outcomes for patients in Georgia. Ultimately pursuing an MD, PhD out of an interest and motivation to realize improved patient outcomes via rigorous discovery science, Collins developed ardent interest in tropical and emerging diseases, particularly vector-borne diseases, which represent a solvable global health problem that imposes a massive disease burden on human populations. This project will focus on a group of closely related viruses called flaviviruses – which includes dengue, Zika, yellow fever, and West Nile – while addressing a fundament gap in knowledge for many viruses: Does a combination of different exposures confer better immunity to a given virus? Leveraging a unique set of human samples, tools, and reagents, we will examine the breadth of flavivirus immunity generated by sequential vaccination with different dengue vaccines and assess how dengue infection impacts the strength and durability of protective immunity to yellow fever. Data generated in this study will have direct implications for ongoing product development as well as public health policy for existing approaches to preventing flavivirus infections. To accomplish the scientific goals of this project and his greater career goals, Dr. Collins has established a comprehensive career development plan that includes connection to a team of advisors, mentors, and collaborators and a focused set of knowledge (vaccinology), skills (biostatistics and trial design) and leadership (team science, academic career development and mentorship) building and training activities. These endeavors are largely accomplished via existing resources available at Emory University and include Clinical Research Bootcamp (Emory), a Vaccinology symposium (Keystone), ADVAC (Advanced course in vaccinology (U of Geneva)), and COMPASS (Career Optimization and Mentoring Program for Academic Success and Support, Emory School of Medicine). Finally, this proposal creates rich opportunities to recruit and mentor trainees in POR. Collins’ commitment to POR has been matched by a strong record of NIH and foundational funding and mentorship. His portfolio of mentees includes undergraduates, masters and doctoral students, medical residents and fellows, and postdocs. This proposal increases Dr. Collins’ abilities and effort in cultivating the next generation of POR investigators. The environment at Emory University affords a deep pool of talented mentees who will benefit from opportunities in team science, interdisciplinary arbovirology, and robust training in experimental design, advanced analyses, and science communication via projects that substantially contribute to ongoing efforts to improve human health and diminish arboviral disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aims to address a fundamental gap in understanding whether a combination of different exposures enhances immunity to dengue virus.
Design and population
The study is an ongoing clinical trial designed to better understand protective immunity to dengue virus, involving human samples and focusing on sequential vaccination with different dengue vaccines.
Core methods
The methods include leveraging a unique set of human samples, tools, and reagents to examine the breadth of flavivirus immunity generated by sequential vaccination and assess the impact of dengue infection on yellow fever immunity.
Main results
The study directly observes the breadth of flavivirus immunity generated by sequential vaccination with different dengue vaccines and evaluates how dengue infection affects the strength and durability of protective immunity to yellow fever.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the findings will contribute to understanding the immune responses to dengue and related flaviviruses, offering novel insights into the potential benefits of combined exposures.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform ongoing product development and public health policy for preventing flavivirus infections, particularly dengue and yellow fever.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on an ongoing clinical trial, which may have limitations in generalizability and control, but the study's design and focus on dengue virus provide a moderate level of confidence in its conclusions.

通过流式细胞术分析虫媒病毒感染组织的细胞群。

Analysis of Cell Populations from Arbovirus-Infected Tissues by Flow Cytometry.
作者： Martins JR, Batista VL, Queiroz-Junior CM, Teixeira MM, Costa VV
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒仍然是世界许多地区公共卫生的重大挑战。尽管具有共同的流行病学特征,但每种虫媒病毒性疾病都表现出不同的细胞向性和激活模式,并发展为尚未完全阐明的免疫病理学结果。要更深入地了解这些过程,需要能够表征通常复杂的细胞反应的方法。在这种背景下,流式细胞术成为一种有价值的工具,可以表征异质细胞群、评估活化和增殖标记、量化炎症介质以及检测细胞死亡途径。通过整合这些参数,流式细胞术可以增强我们对虫媒病毒发病机制的了解,阐明其细胞向性,并了解免疫应答的感染情况。这篇方法论文章概述了将该技术应用于受基孔肯雅热、登革热、黄热病和寨卡病毒感染的不同目标组织的主要步骤,强调了最有效的策略。

问题与背景
虫媒病毒仍然对世界许多地区的公共卫生构成重大挑战,但每种虫媒病毒疾病都表现出独特的细胞向性和激活模式,但尚未完全阐明。
设计与对象
提供的证据未报告研究设计、设置、日期或人群详细信息。

作者将流式细胞术描述为一种工具,可以表征异质细胞群、评估激活和增殖标记、量化炎症介质以及检测细胞死亡途径。
主要结果
提供的证据不报告任何直接观察或定量结果。
解释与创新
作者认为,整合流式细胞术参数可以增强对虫媒病毒 发病机制 的理解,阐明细胞向性,并阐明 免疫应答 的感染作用。
科研与公卫意义
该文章概述了将流式细胞术应用于登革热感染组织的步骤,可能会改进 监测 和诊断方法。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据不报告任何限制或评估证据的强度。

Analysis of Cell Populations from Arbovirus-Infected Tissues by Flow Cytometry.

Authors: Martins JR, Batista VL, Queiroz-Junior CM, Teixeira MM, Costa VV
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses still represent a major challenge to public health in many parts of the world. Despite shared epidemiological characteristics, each arboviral disorder displays distinct cellular tropism and activation patterns, progressing to immunopathological outcomes that remain not fully elucidated. A deeper understanding of these processes requires methodologies capable of characterizing the often-complex cellular responses. In this context, flow cytometry emerges as a valuable tool, enabling the characterization of heterogeneous cell populations, the assessment of activation and proliferation markers, the quantification of inflammatory mediators, and the detection of cell death pathways. By integrating these parameters, flow cytometry can enhance our understanding of arbovirus pathogenesis, clarify their cellular tropism, and understand immune responses to infection. This methodological article outlines the main steps for applying the technique to different target tissues infected by chikungunya, dengue, yellow fever, and Zika viruses, emphasizing the most effective strategies.

Arboviruses still represent a major challenge to public health in many parts of the world, yet each arboviral disorder displays distinct cellular tropism and activation patterns that remain not fully elucidated.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not report the study design, setting, dates, or population details.
Core methods
The authors describe flow cytometry as a tool that enables characterization of heterogeneous cell populations, assessment of activation and proliferation markers, quantification of inflammatory mediators, and detection of cell death pathways.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not report any direct observations or quantitative results.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that integrating flow cytometry parameters can enhance understanding of arbovirus pathogenesis, clarify cellular tropism, and elucidate immune responses to infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The article outlines steps for applying flow cytometry to dengue-infected tissues, potentially improving surveillance and diagnostic approaches.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report any limitations or assess the strength of the evidence.

开发用于快速虫媒病毒检测的单一热循环多重 RT-qPCR 检测方法

Development of a Single Thermal Cycling Multiplex RT-qPCR Assay for Rapid Arbovirus Detection
作者： Róger J. Costa, Andreas Stöcker, Cleyde Sheyla Chachaqui Marconi, Gúbio Soares Campos, Sílvia Inês Sardi, Abevailton Miranda, Carlos Roberto Brites, Wei-Kung Wang, Eduardo Martins Netto
摘要中文翻译背景:具有相似临床表现的多种虫媒病毒的共同循环对公共卫生提出了重大挑战 监测。本研究旨在开发并临床验证一种新型多重 RT-qPCR 检测方法,用于在三个反应中同时检测五种虫媒病毒。方法:在巴西萨尔瓦多进行了一项诊断准确性研究。使用 254 份前瞻性血清样本和 34 份存档的阳性样本进行评估。该检测的引物和探针均通过计算机设计,均针对登革热 (DENV)、寨卡 (ZIKV)、基孔肯雅 (CHIKV)、奥罗普切 (OROV) 和西尼罗河 (WNV) 病毒的高度保守区域。该方案在单个热循环的三个反应中进行了优化,包括一个四重反应(DENV、CHIKV、ZIKV 和 hRNAseP,内源性对照)和两个单重反应(OROV 和 WNV)。结果:在前瞻性分析中,该检测确定了 45 例 DENV、3 例 CHIKV 和 9 例 OROV 阳性病例。DENV 和 CHIKV 的诊断准确率为 96%(95% CI:93.3-98.0%),成功检出参考标准漏检的 4 例 DENV 病例。此外,结合 OROV 可以识别传统三重协议可能忽略的九种情况。结论:这种单循环多重 RT-qPCR 检测将检测范围扩展到传统三重靶标之外,包括 OROV 和 WNV,从而增强了流行病学 监测。这种操作优化缩短了周转时间,并防止对新兴虫媒病毒的诊断不足,成为流行地区的强大诊断工具。

Development of a Single Thermal Cycling Multiplex RT-qPCR Assay for Rapid Arbovirus Detection

Authors: Róger J. Costa, Andreas Stöcker, Cleyde Sheyla Chachaqui Marconi, Gúbio Soares Campos, Sílvia Inês Sardi, Abevailton Miranda, Carlos Roberto Brites, Wei-Kung Wang, Eduardo Martins Netto
Original abstract (English)
Background: The co-circulation of multiple arboviruses with similar clinical presentations poses a significant challenge for public health surveillance. This study aimed to develop and clinically validate a novel multiplex RT-qPCR assay for the simultaneous detection of five arboviruses in three reactions. Methods: A diagnostic accuracy study was conducted in Salvador, Brazil. Evaluation was performed using 254 prospective serum samples and 34 archived positive samples. The primers and probes for the assay were designed in silico, all targeting highly conserved regions of Dengue (DENV), Zika (ZIKV), Chikungunya (CHIKV), Oropouche (OROV), and West Nile (WNV) viruses. The protocol was optimized in three reactions with a single thermal cycle, comprising a tetraplex reaction (DENV, CHIKV, ZIKV, and hRNAseP, an endogenous control) and two singleplex reactions (OROV and WNV). Results: In prospective analysis, the assay identified 45 DENV, 3 CHIKV, and 9 OROV-positive cases. Diagnostic accuracy for DENV and CHIKV was 96% (95% CI: 93.3–98.0%), successfully detecting four DENV cases missed by the reference standard. Furthermore, incorporating OROV enabled the identification of nine cases that conventional triplex protocols would have overlooked. Conclusion: This single-cycling multiplex RT-qPCR assay enhances epidemiological surveillance by expanding detection beyond traditional triplex targets to include OROV and WNV. This operational optimization reduces turnaround times and prevents the underdiagnosis of emerging arboviruses, serving as a robust diagnostic tool for endemic regions.

社论:传染病的数学建模和数据分析

Editorial: Mathematical Modelling and data analysis in infectious diseases
作者： Omame A, Xiao Y, Han Q, Iyaniwura S
摘要中文翻译这些论文共同强化了一个统一的信息:没有一种方法足以解决所有问题,正确的分析方法必须与当前的决策、数据生成过程以及推理错误的实际后果相匹配。它的统一信息很简单:没有一种方法足以解决所有问题。相反,方法的选择应该根据手头的决策、生成数据的过程、实际可用的观察结果以及不正确的推理或预测的后果来确定。他们的工作强调了实用的可识别性和数值稳定性,是将机械模型转化为可用于公共卫生决策的工具的先决条件。补充预测流将经典时间序列方法与非线性学习相结合。研究表明,社区间流动和基于学校的接触可以增加 暴发 概率并降低干预措施的有效性,而暴露后预防和主动病例发现的结合使用可大大降低跨场景的 暴发 风险。摘要本研究主题汇集了 22 项贡献,展示了目前用于理解和应对传染病威胁的数学和计算工具的广度。该研究主题涵盖机械建模、统计预测、机器学习、随机优化、计算流体动力学和负担分析,涵盖从 COVID-19 和流感到麻疹、腮腺炎和登革热等疾病。贡献集中在三个中心主题:从嘈杂的实时 监测 数据中提取稳健的、与决策相关的预测;模拟干预措施、移动性和连通性如何影响...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了对强大的数学模型和数据分析工具的需求,以理解和模拟传染病,特别是那些公共卫生问题,如登革热、流感和 COVID-19。该研究旨在改进 监测、预测和干预策略。
设计与对象
研究设计涉及广泛的方法,包括应用于各种疾病的机械建模、统计预测、机器学习和计算流体动力学。人口包括来自 基因组监测、移动轨迹和电子健康记录的数据。
核心方法
这些方法包括机械建模、统计预测、机器学习、随机优化、计算流体动力学和负荷分析。这些方法适用于登​​革热、流感和 COVID-19 等疾病。
主要结果
结果表明,混合模型可以提高预测准确性,并且干预措施必须针对具体的连通结构(例如铁路网络、建筑物和社会联系)进行定制。这些研究还强调了背景协变量在疾病负担估计中的重要性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为展示了整合各种数据源和建模技术以改善公共卫生决策的价值。新颖的贡献包括混合模型的开发以及环境、社会经济和卫生系统数据的整合。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过提高疾病预测的准确性和干预措施的有效性来为 监测 实践提供信息。他们还强调了疾病负担方面持续存在的不平等以及采取综合 监测 和 疫苗接种 战略的必要性。
局限与证据强度
局限性包括需要对混合模型进行前瞻性评估以及对生态和预测关联进行仔细解释。由于涉及的疾病范围和方法多种多样,证据强度中等。

Editorial: Mathematical Modelling and data analysis in infectious diseases

Authors: Omame A, Xiao Y, Han Q, Iyaniwura S
Original abstract (English)
Together, these papers reinforce a unifying message: no single method suffices for every question, and the right analytical approach must be matched to the decision at hand, the data-generating process, and the practical consequences of getting the inference wrong. Its unifying message is simple: no single method suffices for every question. Instead, the choice of method should follow from the decision at hand, the process generating the data, the observations actually available, and the consequences of incorrect inference or prediction. Their work underscores practical identifiability and numerical stability as prerequisites for translating mechanistic models into tools that can be used operationally in public health decision-making.A complementary forecasting stream combines classical time-series methods with nonlinear learning. showed that inter-community mobility and school-based contact can increase outbreak probability and diminish the effectiveness of interventions, while the combined use of post-exposure prophylaxis and active case finding substantially reduces outbreak risk across scenarios.At a finer spatial scale, Chong et al. SummaryThis Research Topic brings together 22 contributions that showcase the breadth of mathematical and computational tools now used to understand and respond to infectious disease threats. Spanning mechanistic modeling, statistical forecasting, machine learning, stochastic optimization, computational fluid dynamics, and burden analysis, the Research Topic addresses diseases ranging from COVID-19 and influenza to measles, mumps, and dengue. Contributions cluster around three central themes: extracting robust, decision-relevant forecasts from noisy real-time surveillance data; modeling how interventions, mobility, and connectivity sha...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for robust mathematical models and data analysis tools to understand and simulate infectious diseases, particularly those of public health concern, such as dengue, influenza, and COVID-19. The research aims to improve surveillance, forecasting, and intervention strategies.
Design and population
The study design involves a wide range of approaches, including mechanistic modeling, statistical forecasting, machine learning, and computational fluid dynamics, applied across various diseases. The population includes data from genomic surveillance, mobility traces, and electronic health records.
Core methods
The methods include mechanistic modeling, statistical forecasting, machine learning, stochastic optimization, computational fluid dynamics, and burden analyses. These methods are applied to diseases such as dengue, influenza, and COVID-19, among others.
Main results
The results show that hybrid models can improve prediction accuracy, and that interventions must be tailored to the specific structure of connectivity, such as rail networks, buildings, and social contacts. The studies also highlight the importance of contextual covariates in disease burden estimation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating the value of integrating various data sources and modeling techniques to improve public health decision-making. The novel contributions include the development of hybrid models and the integration of environmental, socioeconomic, and health-system data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance practice by improving the accuracy of disease forecasting and the effectiveness of interventions. They also highlight persistent inequities in disease burden and the need for integrated surveillance and vaccination strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The limitations include the need for prospective evaluation of hybrid models and careful interpretation of ecological and predictive associations. The evidence strength is moderate due to the diverse range of diseases and methods involved.

多功能泛 DENV NS5 多克隆抗体的生产和免疫原性能的结构表位分析

Production of a Versatile Pan-DENV NS5 Polyclonal Antibody and Structural Epitope Analysis of Immunogen Performance
作者： Liliana Morales, Claudia Bueno-Angarita, María Angélica Calderón-Peláez, Myriam L. Velandia-Romero, Jaime E. Castellanos, Jacqueline Chaparro-Olaya
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种针对 DENV NS5 保守区域的多功能多克隆抗体,用于免疫检测应用,解决缺乏适合各种检测的广泛反应性抗 NS5 抗体的问题。
设计与对象
该研究涉及在大肠杆菌中表达 DENV2 NS5 的甲基转移酶和 RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶结构域的保守重组区域,并将它们用作小鼠模型中的免疫原。
核心方法
DENV2 NS5 的甲基转移酶和 RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶结构域的保守重组区域在大肠杆菌中表达,并用作小鼠模型中的免疫原。
主要结果
抗 NS5-RdRp 抗体在蛋白质印迹中检测到所有四种登革热血清型的 NS5,其非特异性背景低于广泛使用的商业抗体,并且在 DENV1 和 DENV2 感染细胞的免疫荧光和免疫沉淀测定中有效。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,抗 NS5-RdRp 抗体是登革热研究的宝贵工具,说明结构表位分析如何能够深入了解免疫原性能的差异。
科研与公卫意义
抗 NS5-RdRp 抗体可用于登革热研究和 诊断,有可能改善 登革病毒 与 宿主 细胞相互作用的检测和理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到使用单一 DENV2 NS5 免疫原的限制,该免疫原可能无法完全代表所有登革热血清型中 NS5 蛋白的多样性。

Production of a Versatile Pan-DENV NS5 Polyclonal Antibody and Structural Epitope Analysis of Immunogen Performance

Authors: Liliana Morales, Claudia Bueno-Angarita, María Angélica Calderón-Peláez, Myriam L. Velandia-Romero, Jaime E. Castellanos, Jacqueline Chaparro-Olaya
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue is a major mosquito-borne viral disease worldwide, with approximately 400 million infections reported annually and no fully effective antiviral treatments available. The non-structural protein NS5 is a promising antiviral target due to its critical role in viral RNA replication and host interactions. Although commercial monoclonal and polyclonal anti-NS5 antibodies are available, none were found to combine broad reactivity against all four dengue virus serotypes with suitability for Western Blot (WB), Immunofluorescence (IF), and Immunoprecipitation (IP) assays. This study aimed to develop and functionally characterize a versatile polyclonal antibody targeting a conserved region of DENV NS5 for immunodetection applications. Methods: Conserved recombinant regions from the methyltransferase (MTase) and RNA-dependent RNA polymerase (RdRp) domains of DENV2 NS5 were expressed in Escherichia coli and used as immunogens in a murine model. Results: Both recombinant proteins exhibited predicted antigenic potential, but only the anti-NS5-RdRp antibody efficiently recognized native NS5 in infected cells. The resulting antibody detected NS5 from all four dengue serotypes in WB, with lower nonspecific background than a widely used commercial antibody. In addition, it proved effective in IF and IP assays in DENV1- and DENV2-infected cells, enabling co-immuno-precipitation of NS3, a known NS5 interactor. Retrospective structural epitope analyses suggested that the RdRp immunogen contained surface-accessible conformational features more compatible with antibody recognition in the native protein context. Conclusion: These findings identify the anti-NS5-RdRp antibody as a valuable tool for dengue research and illustrate how structural epitope analyses can provide insights into differences in immunogen performance
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a versatile polyclonal antibody targeting a conserved region of DENV NS5 for immunodetection applications, addressing the lack of broad-reactive anti-NS5 antibodies suitable for various assays.
Design and population
The study involved expressing conserved recombinant regions from the methyltransferase and RNA-dependent RNA polymerase domains of DENV2 NS5 in Escherichia coli and using them as immunogens in a murine model.
Core methods
Conserved recombinant regions from the methyltransferase and RNA-dependent RNA polymerase domains of DENV2 NS5 were expressed in Escherichia coli and used as immunogens in a murine model.
Main results
The anti-NS5-RdRp antibody detected NS5 from all four dengue serotypes in Western Blot with lower nonspecific background than a widely used commercial antibody, and it was effective in Immunofluorescence and Immunoprecipitation assays in DENV1- and DENV2-infected cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings identify the anti-NS5-RdRp antibody as a valuable tool for dengue research, illustrating how structural epitope analyses can provide insights into differences in immunogen performance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The anti-NS5-RdRp antibody can be used for dengue research and diagnosis, potentially improving the detection and understanding of dengue virus interactions with host cells.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a single DENV2 NS5 immunogen, which may not fully represent the diversity of NS5 proteins across all dengue serotypes.

洪水后因急性发热性疾病住院患者的临床特征和探索性结果关联:来自越南的多中心 队列研究 研究

Clinical characteristics and exploratory outcome associations among patients hospitalized with acute febrile illness during a post-flood period: a multicenter cohort study from Vietnam
作者： Le T, Tran T, Bui N, Kieu H, Ma T, Dong H
摘要中文翻译这项研究描述了越南北部洪水后时期患有急性发热性疾病的住院患者,探讨了与不良短期结果的关联。这些发现代表了受洪水影响社区的患者之间的 队列研究 内关联,并不表明洪水导致了疾病发作或结果。材料和方法研究设计和报告指南这项多中心回顾性观察性 队列研究 研究描述了 2025 年 10 月至 11 月越南太原省洪水后时期患有急性发热性疾病的住院患者。该研究评估了人口特征、记录的环境指标、临床和入院实验室检查结果、记录的病因诊断、抗生素使用、疾病严重程度和 治疗 反应。补充表S1提供了完整的清单。研究设置连续从太原中心医院、A医院、太原医院和太原医药大学医院招募患者。这项研究是在当地发生了一场重大洪水事件之后进行的,该事件造成了不同程度的影响,从暂时的水覆盖到大面积洪水导致护理服务和水卫生系统中断。摘要洪水会破坏卫生、污染水源并改变传染病的模式;然而,根据基于医院的回顾性数据,很难将个体发热性疾病事件归因于洪水暴露。这项多中心回顾性观察 队列研究 包括 2025 年 10 月至 11 月在太原省收治的 237 名连续患者。人口统计、记录的环境、临床、实验室、病因和 队列研究 数据从医疗记录中获得...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨洪水后环境因素与因急性发热性疾病住院患者不良结局之间的关联,解决在回顾性医院数据中将个体发热事件归因于洪水暴露的挑战。
设计与对象
2025 年 10 月至 11 月在越南太原省进行了一项多中心回顾性观察性 队列研究 研究,纳入了 237 名在洪水后时期因急性发热性疾病入院的连续患者。患者来自太原中央医院、A 医院和太原医药大学医院。
核心方法
使用标准化的 病例报告 表格从电子病历中回顾性地提取基线人口统计和临床特征。单独的单变量逻辑回归模型使用比值比、95% 置信区间和两侧 p 值检查了与严重疾病和 治疗 无应答的关联。入院时获得实验室值,并测量从症状出现到入院期间的发烧持续时间。
主要结果
Severe disease occurred in 8.4% of patients, and treatment non-response was observed in 6.8%. Exploratory univariable associations showed significant links between treatment non-response and place of residence (OR 1.027, 95% CI 1.027-20.880, p=0.030), water use during flooding (OR 1.228, 95% CI 1.228-10.897, p=0.014), and chronic disease history (OR 2.386, 95% CI 2.386-20.747, p=0.001).
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 队列研究 内关联,而不是洪水和发热性疾病之间的因果关系,强调结果的探索性和进一步调查的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可能为受洪水影响的社区的 监测 和风险评估策略提供信息,强调与严重疾病和急性发热性疾病无反应相关的潜在环境和临床因素。
局限与证据强度
这些分析是假设生成的,容易受到稀疏数据不稳定和混杂的影响,并且没有对多重比较进行调整。由于探索性且缺乏明确的因果结论,证据是中等的。

Clinical characteristics and exploratory outcome associations among patients hospitalized with acute febrile illness during a post-flood period: a multicenter cohort study from Vietnam

Authors: Le T, Tran T, Bui N, Kieu H, Ma T, Dong H
Original abstract (English)
This study described hospitalized patients with acute febrile illness during a post-flood period in northern Vietnam, exploring associations with adverse short-term outcomes. These findings represent within-cohort associations among patients from flood-affected communities and do not indicate that flooding caused the illness episodes or outcomes. MATERIALS AND METHODSStudy design and reporting guidelineThis multicenter retrospective observational cohort study described hospitalized patients with acute febrile illness during a post-flood period in Thai Nguyen province, Vietnam, from October to November 2025. The study evaluated demographic characteristics, documented environmental indicators, clinical and admission laboratory findings, recorded etiologic diagnoses, antibiotic utilization, disease severity, and treatment response. Supplementary Table S1 provides a complete checklist.Study settingPatients were recruited consecutively from Thai Nguyen Central Hospital, Hospital A, Thai Nguyen, and Thai Nguyen University of Medicine and Pharmacy Hospital. The study followed a major local flooding event with heterogeneous documented impacts, ranging from temporary water coverage to extensive inundation with disruption of care access and water-sanitation systems. ABSTRACTFlooding can disrupt sanitation, contaminate water sources, and alter patterns of infectious disease; however, causal attribution of an individual febrile illness episode to flood exposure is difficult in retrospective hospital-based data. This multicenter retrospective observational cohort included 237 consecutive patients admitted in Thai Nguyen province from October to November 2025. Demographic, documented environmental, clinical, laboratory, etiologic, and treatment data were obtained from medical record...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore associations between post-flood environmental factors and adverse outcomes in patients hospitalized with acute febrile illness, addressing the challenge of attributing individual febrile episodes to flood exposure in retrospective hospital-based data.
Design and population
A multicenter retrospective observational cohort study was conducted in Thai Nguyen province, Vietnam, from October to November 2025, including 237 consecutive patients admitted with acute febrile illness during a post-flood period. Patients were recruited from Thai Nguyen Central Hospital, Hospital A, and Thai Nguyen University of Medicine and Pharmacy Hospital.
Core methods
Baseline demographic and clinical characteristics were extracted retrospectively from electronic medical records using a standardized case report form. Separate univariable logistic regression models examined associations with severe disease and treatment non-response, using odds ratios, 95% confidence intervals, and two-sided p-values. Laboratory values were obtained at hospital admission, and fever duration was measured from symptom onset to admission.
Main results
Severe disease occurred in 8.4% of patients, and treatment non-response was observed in 6.8%. Exploratory univariable associations showed significant links between treatment non-response and place of residence (OR 1.027, 95% CI 1.027-20.880, p=0.030), water use during flooding (OR 1.228, 95% CI 1.228-10.897, p=0.014), and chronic disease history (OR 2.386, 95% CI 2.386-20.747, p=0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as within-cohort associations rather than causal links between flooding and febrile illness, emphasizing the exploratory nature of the results and the need for further investigation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform surveillance and risk assessment strategies in flood-affected communities, highlighting potential environmental and clinical factors associated with severe disease and treatment non-response in acute febrile illness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The analyses were hypothesis-generating and vulnerable to sparse-data instability and confounding, with no adjustment for multiple comparisons. The evidence is moderate due to the exploratory nature and lack of definitive causal conclusions.

从 IL-6 产生到 IL-6R 缓冲:病毒感染期间免疫稳态的统一 IL-6-IL-6R 框架

From IL-6 production to IL-6R buffering: a unified IL-6-IL-6R framework for immune homeostasis during viral infection
作者： Yousif A
摘要中文翻译在这篇综述中,我们整合了来自人类遗传学、细胞因子生物学、受体信号传导和登革热免疫发病机制的证据,提出了一个统一的、假设驱动的 IL-6 调控框架。在这篇综述中,我们提出IL-6和IL-6R内的遗传变异代表了细胞因子调节的两个互补维度:IL-6变异主要影响IL-6产生的程度,而IL-6R变异可能通过可溶性受体动力学改变细胞因子的持久性和生物利用度。白介素 6 (IL-6) 是一种多效细胞因子,可协调先天性和适应性免疫,并在感染和炎症期间维持组织稳态。尽管短暂的 IL-6 产生对于 宿主 防御至关重要,但失调或持续的 IL-6 活性有助于传染性和炎症性疾病的免疫病理学。登革病毒 (DENV) 感染提供了一个可能破坏这种平衡的重要背景。循环 IL-6 水平升高与内皮功能障碍、血管渗漏、凝血病和 重症登革热 相关;然而,调节 IL-6 生物利用度、信号传导持续时间和分辨率的机制仍不完全清楚。IL6 和 IL6R 内的遗传变异可能影响 IL-6 调节的不同但相互关联的层面。内含子 IL6 rs2069843 和启动子 rs1800795 变体与某些人群的登革热易感性和严重程度相关,并被认为会影响 IL-6 表达,尽管它们的作用并未得到一致的复制。相比之下,功能性 IL6R rs2228145(Asp358Ala;也称为 rs8192284)变体增强膜结合 IL-6R 的脱落并增加循环可溶性 IL-6R (sIL-6R),从而改变 IL-6 信号传导的细胞外环境。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 IL-6 和 IL-6R 相互作用如何在病毒感染期间调节免疫稳态,特别是在登革热情况下,IL-6 活性失调会导致严重的疾病结果。
设计与对象
本综述整合了来自人类遗传学、细胞因子生物学、受体信号传导和登革热免疫发病机制的证据,提出了急性病毒感染期间 IL-6 调节的统一框架,重点关注 登革病毒 及其相关疾病表现。
核心方法
作者综合了遗传研究、细胞因子生物学和受体信号传导机制(包括 IL6 和 IL6R 变体的作用)的发现,以开发登革热感染期间 IL-6 调节的假设驱动模型。
主要结果
该综述强调,可溶性 IL-6R (sIL-6R) 的增加可能会促进 IL-6 反式信号传导或有助于细胞外缓冲,具体取决于可溶性 gp130 (sgp130) 的可用性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,IL-6 产生、sIL-6R 可用性和 sgp130 介导的中和之间的动态平衡比单独循环 IL-6 浓度更有效地决定了 IL-6 活性的强度和持续时间。
科研与公卫意义
该框架可能为未来生物标志物的开发和精确的免疫调节策略提供信息,以管理登革热和具有类似炎症机制的其他急性病毒感染。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项综述,没有提供原始实验数据,这限制了证据的强度。然而,它整合了多个研究领域,提出了一个新颖的假设。

From IL-6 production to IL-6R buffering: a unified IL-6-IL-6R framework for immune homeostasis during viral infection

Authors: Yousif A
Original abstract (English)
In this review, we integrate evidence from human genetics, cytokine biology, receptor signaling, and dengue immunopathogenesis to propose a unified, hypothesis-driven framework of IL-6 regulation. In this review, we propose that genetic variation within IL-6 and IL-6R represents two complementary dimensions of cytokine regulation: IL-6 variants primarily influence the magnitude of IL-6 production, whereas IL-6R variants may modify cytokine persistence and bioavailability through soluble receptor dynamics. Interleukin-6 (IL-6) is a pleiotropic cytokine that coordinates innate and adaptive immunity and maintains tissue homeostasis during infection and inflammation. Although transient IL-6 production is essential for host defense, dysregulated or sustained IL-6 activity contributes to immunopathology across infectious and inflammatory diseases. Dengue virus (DENV) infection provides an important context in which this balance may become disrupted. Elevated circulating IL-6 levels are associated with endothelial dysfunction, vascular leakage, coagulopathy, and severe dengue; however, the mechanisms regulating IL-6 bioavailability, signaling duration, and resolution remain incompletely understood. Genetic variation within IL6 and IL6R may influence distinct but interconnected layers of IL-6 regulation. The intronic IL6 rs2069843 and promoter rs1800795 variants have been associated with dengue susceptibility and severity in some populations and proposed to influence IL-6 expression, although their effects are not consistently replicated. In contrast, the functional IL6R rs2228145 (Asp358Ala; also known as rs8192284) variant enhances shedding of membrane-bound IL-6R and increases circulating soluble IL-6R (sIL-6R), thereby altering the extracellular context of IL-6 signaling....
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how IL-6 and IL-6R interactions regulate immune homeostasis during viral infection, particularly in the context of dengue, where dysregulated IL-6 activity contributes to severe disease outcomes.
Design and population
This review integrates evidence from human genetics, cytokine biology, receptor signaling, and dengue immunopathogenesis to propose a unified framework for IL-6 regulation during acute viral infection, focusing on dengue virus and its associated disease manifestations.
Core methods
The authors synthesized findings from genetic studies, cytokine biology, and receptor signaling mechanisms, including the role of IL6 and IL6R variants, to develop a hypothesis-driven model of IL-6 regulation during dengue infection.
Main results
The review highlights that increased soluble IL-6R (sIL-6R) may either facilitate IL-6 trans-signaling or contribute to extracellular buffering depending on the availability of soluble gp130 (sgp130).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the dynamic balance among IL-6 production, sIL-6R availability, and sgp130-mediated neutralization determines the intensity and duration of IL-6 activity more effectively than circulating IL-6 concentration alone.
Scientific and public-health significance
This framework may inform future biomarker development and precision immunomodulatory strategies for managing dengue disease and other acute viral infections with similar inflammatory mechanisms.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a review and does not present original experimental data, which limits the strength of the evidence. However, it integrates multiple lines of research to propose a novel hypothesis.

气候变化如何影响蚊媒传染病:疟疾和登革热的不同轨迹

How climate change affects mosquito-borne infectious disease: The different trajectories of malaria and dengue
作者： Toby Borland Cumming, Henry Surendra, Karin S Leder, Katherine L. Anders
摘要中文翻译随着气候变化导致气温升高,额外的温暖加上降雨和湿度,改善了蚊子种群繁衍的条件。按蚊和伊蚊 媒介 的更大传播增加了 传播 的风险,传播 是世界上最流行的蚊媒传染病、疟疾和登革热。然而,气候驱动因素对疾病的影响是复杂的:关系是非线性的,影响是季节性的,并且存在很大的局部变异性。影响疾病的许多非气候因素进一步增加了复杂性,包括人员流动、人口增长、免疫水平、土地利用变化、城市化、控制干预措施和医疗保健系统的质量。在这里,我们解释了为什么尽管气候驱动因素对疟疾和登革热 传播 参数具有大致相似的影响,但这两种疾病的轨迹却不同,登革热迅速增长,但疟疾长期下降。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了气候变化如何影响蚊媒传染病,特别关注 登革病毒 和登革热。它探讨了气候驱动因素与疾病 传播 之间复杂的相互作用,强调了疟疾和登革热的不同轨迹。
证据基础与方法
该综述系统分析了气候变化对蚊媒疾病的影响,重点关注登革热。它讨论了温度、降雨量和湿度在增加蚊子数量和疾病传播方面的作用。该综述还概述了非气候因素对疾病 传播 的影响。
共识与结论
气候变化对疟疾和登革热 传播 参数的影响大致相似,但两种疾病的发展轨迹不同。尽管受到共同的环境影响,登革热正在快速增长,而疟疾则呈现长期下降趋势。
争议与缺口
该综述承认,气候驱动因素对疾病的影响是复杂的,具有非线性关系、季节性影响和显着的局部变异性。它还强调了非气候因素的影响,这些因素可能掩盖或改变与气候相关的趋势,从而造成证据差距。
科研与实践启示
审查表明需要对 监测 动态进行有针对性的研究和 监测,特别是在经历气候变化的地区。它强调了将气候数据与公共卫生战略相结合以应对日益严重的登革热威胁的重要性。

How climate change affects mosquito-borne infectious disease: The different trajectories of malaria and dengue

Authors: Toby Borland Cumming, Henry Surendra, Karin S Leder, Katherine L. Anders
Original abstract (English)
As climate change increases temperatures, additional warmth combines with rainfall and humidity to enhance conditions for mosquito populations to thrive. Greater spread of Anopheles and Aedes vectors increases the risk of transmission of the world’s most prevalent mosquito-borne infectious diseases, malaria and dengue. Yet the impacts of climate drivers on disease transmission are complex: Relationships are nonlinear, effects are seasonal, and there is substantial local variability. Further complexity is added by the many nonclimate factors that influence disease transmission, including movement of people, population growth, immunity levels, land use change, urbanization, control interventions, and quality of health care systems. Here, we explain why, despite climate drivers having broadly similar effects on malaria and dengue transmission parameters, the trajectories of the two diseases differ, with rapid growth in dengue but long-term decline in malaria.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines how climate change affects mosquito-borne infectious diseases, specifically focusing on dengue virus and dengue disease. It explores the complex interactions between climate drivers and disease transmission, highlighting the differing trajectories of malaria and dengue.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of climate change impacts on mosquito-borne diseases, with a focus on dengue. It discusses the role of temperature, rainfall, and humidity in enhancing mosquito populations and the spread of diseases. The review also outlines the influence of nonclimate factors on disease transmission.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that climate change has broadly similar effects on malaria and dengue transmission parameters, but the trajectories of the two diseases differ. Dengue is experiencing rapid growth, while malaria is showing a long-term decline, despite shared environmental influences.
Controversies and gaps
The review acknowledges that the impacts of climate drivers on disease transmission are complex, with nonlinear relationships, seasonal effects, and substantial local variability. It also highlights the influence of nonclimate factors, which can obscure or modify climate-related trends, creating evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests the need for targeted research and surveillance on dengue virus dynamics, particularly in regions experiencing climate change. It emphasizes the importance of integrating climate data with public health strategies to address the growing threat of dengue.

2020年至2026年虫媒病毒风险变化及应对措施

Changes in arbovirus risks and responses from 2020 to 2026
作者： Z. Madewell, L. Adams, S. Guagliardo, Anna Drexler, Roberto Barrera, R. Nett, G. Paz-Bailey, N. Dean, Zachary J. Madewell, Laura E. Adams
摘要中文翻译摘要 自 2020 年以来,通过蚊子和蠓传播的病毒已成为一个更大且更难以预测的公共卫生挑战。登革热继续造成最大的负担,但基孔肯雅热、奥罗普切热和其他病毒正在重新出现或传播到新的地区。在这篇叙述性综述中,我们总结了 2020 年至 2026 年虫媒病毒 流行病学、监测、诊断、疫苗接种、治疗、媒介 生态和蚊虫控制的重大变化。新工具可以帮助及早发现风险并预防疾病,但其有用性取决于当地能力、安全监测、可负担性、环境考虑和持续实施。虫媒病毒控制的下一阶段将取决于在 暴发 压垮卫生系统之前战略性地、公平地使用这些工具。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
简介 节肢动物传播的病毒,特别是登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒、黄热病和西尼罗河病毒,在 COVID-19 时代仍然是主要的传染威胁。
证据基础与方法
在这篇叙述性综述中,我们总结了 2020 年至 2026 年虫媒病毒 流行病学、监测、诊断、疫苗接种、治疗、媒介 生态和蚊虫控制的主要变化。
共识与结论
热带地区的大多数大城市以及温带地区越来越多的大城市现在面临着不断变化的虫媒病毒威胁。
争议与缺口
第三,针对登革热、黄热病和基孔肯雅热的 疫苗接种 政策越来越多地受到实施和安全监测的影响,而治疗方法仍然有限。
科研与实践启示
其次,监测 和诊断变得更加具有前瞻性,环境、数字和基于实验室的工具补充了常规临床和公共卫生报告。

Changes in arbovirus risks and responses from 2020 to 2026

Authors: Z. Madewell, L. Adams, S. Guagliardo, Anna Drexler, Roberto Barrera, R. Nett, G. Paz-Bailey, N. Dean, Zachary J. Madewell, Laura E. Adams
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Since 2020, viruses spread by mosquitoes and biting midges have become a larger and less predictable public health challenge. Dengue continues to cause the greatest burden, but chikungunya, Oropouche, and other viruses are re-emerging or spreading into new areas. In this narrative review, we summarize major changes in arbovirus epidemiology, surveillance, diagnosis, vaccination, treatment, vector ecology, and mosquito control from 2020 to 2026. New tools can help detect risk earlier and prevent disease, but their usefulness depends on local capacity, safety monitoring, affordability, environmental considerations, and sustained implementation. The next phase of arbovirus control will depend on using these tools strategically, equitably, and before outbreaks overwhelm health systems.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Introduction Arthropod-borne viruses, particularly dengue, chikungunya, Zika, yellow fever, and West Nile, remained major infectious threats during the COVID-19 era.
Evidence base and method
In this narrative review, we summarize major changes in arbovirus epidemiology, surveillance, diagnosis, vaccination, treatment, vector ecology, and mosquito control from 2020 to 2026.
Consensus and conclusions
Most large cities in the tropics, and an increasing number in temperate zones, now face a shifting mix of arboviral threats.
Controversies and gaps
Third, vaccination policy for dengue, yellow fever, and chikungunya is increasingly shaped by implementation and safety monitoring, while therapeutics remain limited.
Research and practice implications
Second, surveillance and diagnostics are becoming more anticipatory, with environmental, digital, and laboratory-based tools complementing routine clinical and public health reporting.

登革病毒 血清型对伊蚊中肠微生物群的调节:宿主-病原体-微生物组相互作用的综述 登革热病毒与伊蚊肠道中微生物群的调节: una revisión de las interacciones hospedador-patógeno-microbioma

Modulation of Aedes Midgut Microbiota by Dengue Virus Serotypes: A Review of Host-Pathogen-Microbiome Interactions Modulación de la microbiota del intestino medio de Aedes por los serotipos del virus del dengue: una revisión de las interacciones hospedador-patógeno-microbioma
作者： Manuel Alvarez Troncoso Corbacho, Jamille França Araújo, Maicon Souza Fróis dos Santos, Carol Santos Lemos, Kauan Pablo Sales Fernandes, Henrique Leonardi Cardôso, Joilson Pereira Alves Júnior, Fabrício Freire de Melo
摘要中文翻译埃及伊蚊中肠微生物群是 媒介 能力的关键决定因素,调节对 登革病毒 (DENV) 的免疫和代谢反应。病毒感染在这种环境中引发了明显的生态变化,挑战了细菌“核心”对抗血粉引起的生态失调的稳定性。这些微生物动态是由病毒血清型、感染后时间、蚊子生理状态、血粉来源和实验条件之间复杂的相互作用决定的。虽然基础核心通常持续存在,但据报道,特定细菌家族表现出与血清型相关的波动,并且可能代表病毒易感性或耐药性的候选相关性。阐明这些 宿主-病原体-微生物组相互作用对于定义 宿主 的生物屏障至关重要。这篇叙述性综述综合了 DENV 血清型 1-4 对蚊子微生物组调节的当前证据,强调了生态细微差别,并确定了值得进一步研究的细菌分类群和机制,作为基于微生物组的 媒介 控制的潜在目标。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了 登革病毒 血清型如何调节埃及伊蚊中肠微生物群及其对 媒介 能力和 传播 屏障(A1、A2、A3、A5)的影响。
证据基础与方法
证据描述了叙述性综述 (A6),但没有报告数据库、检索日期、资格标准、选择方法或纳入的研究计数。
共识与结论
该综述的结论是,尽管感染,中肠细菌核心通常仍然存在,血清型特异性细菌家族波动可能与病毒易感性或耐药性相关,并且这些 宿主-病原体-微生物组相互作用定义了 传播 障碍,并强调了未来 媒介 控制的分类单元/机制(A4、A5、A6)。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据未报告争议或证据差距(A1、A2、A3、A4、A5、A6)。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应侧重于确定由 DENV 血清型调节的特定细菌分类群和机制,以开发基于微生物组的 媒介 控制干预措施并澄清 传播 障碍(A5、A6)。

Modulation of Aedes Midgut Microbiota by Dengue Virus Serotypes: A Review of Host-Pathogen-Microbiome Interactions Modulación de la microbiota del intestino medio de Aedes por los serotipos del virus del dengue: una revisión de las interacciones hospedador-patógeno-microbioma

Authors: Manuel Alvarez Troncoso Corbacho, Jamille França Araújo, Maicon Souza Fróis dos Santos, Carol Santos Lemos, Kauan Pablo Sales Fernandes, Henrique Leonardi Cardôso, Joilson Pereira Alves Júnior, Fabrício Freire de Melo
Original abstract (English)
The Aedes aegypti midgut microbiota serves as a critical determinant of vector competence, modulating immune and metabolic responses to Dengue virus (DENV). Viral infection precipitates distinct ecological shifts within this environment, challenging the stability of the bacterial “core” against blood-meal induced dysbiosis. These microbial dynamics are shaped by a complex interplay between viral serotypes, time post-infection, mosquito physiological status, blood-meal source, and experimental conditions. While the basal core often persists, specific bacterial families have been reported to show serotype-associated fluctuations and may represent candidate correlates of viral susceptibility or resistance. Elucidating these host-pathogen-microbiome interactions is essential for defining biological barriers to transmission. This narrative review synthesizes current evidence on the modulation of the mosquito microbiome by DENV serotypes 1-4, highlighting ecological nuances and identifying bacterial taxa and mechanisms that warrant further investigation as potential targets for microbiome-based vector control.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines how dengue virus serotypes modulate the Aedes aegypti midgut microbiota and its effect on vector competence and transmission barriers (A1, A2, A3, A5).
Evidence base and method
The evidence describes a narrative review (A6) without reporting databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, selection method, or included study count.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the midgut bacterial core generally persists despite infection, that serotype‐specific bacterial family fluctuations may serve as correlates of viral susceptibility or resistance, and that these host‐pathogen‐microbiome interactions define transmission barriers and highlight taxa/mechanisms for future vector control (A4, A5, A6).
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report controversies or evidence gaps (A1, A2, A3, A4, A5, A6).
Research and practice implications
Future research should focus on identifying specific bacterial taxa and mechanisms modulated by DENV serotypes to develop microbiome‐based vector control interventions and clarify transmission barriers (A5, A6).

登革热之外:继发性免疫性血小板减少症表现为持续性皮肤粘膜出血 - A 病例报告 和叙述回顾

Beyond Dengue: Secondary Immune Thrombocytopenia Presenting as Persistent Mucocutaneous Bleeding-A Case Report and Narrative Review
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注表现为持续性皮肤粘膜出血的继发性免疫性血小板减少症(ITP),特别是在登革热感染的情况下。核心问题是登革热后持续性血小板减少是否应归因于残留病毒效应或免疫介导的过程。
证据基础与方法
该评论是叙述性评论和病例报告,而不是系统分析或荟萃分析。它包括一名患有登革热感染并出现继发性 ITP 的患者的单个案例研究。证据来自临床观察和实验室结果,没有提及数据库、检索日期或研究纳入的资格标准。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,登革热感染后继发性 ITP 并不常见,但具有临床意义。它强调登革热后持续性血小板减少不应自动归因于残留病毒效应。ITP 的 发病机制 被描述为涉及免疫功能障碍,包括自身抗体介导的血小板破坏、脾巨噬细胞吞噬作用和 T 细胞介导的破坏。该病例支持免疫介导的过程,而不是直接的病毒效应。
争议与缺口
审查指出,由于缺乏长期随访数据,无法最终确定登革热感染与 ITP 之间的明确因果关系。它还强调需要进一步研究以确定登革热后 ITP 的 发病率、风险因素和最佳管理策略。
科研与实践启示
该综述建议,对于登革热感染后出现持续性血小板减少和出血表现的患者,临床医生应对继发性 ITP 保持高度怀疑。它建议进行全面评估并及时进行免疫抑制治疗,以降低发病率并改善患者预后。

Beyond Dengue: Secondary Immune Thrombocytopenia Presenting as Persistent Mucocutaneous Bleeding-A Case Report and Narrative Review

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)

View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on secondary immune thrombocytopenia (ITP) presenting as persistent mucocutaneous bleeding, specifically in the context of dengue infection. The central question is whether persistent thrombocytopenia after dengue should be attributed to residual viral effects or an immune-mediated process.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative review and case report, not a systematic or meta-analysis. It includes a single case study of a patient with dengue infection who developed secondary ITP. The evidence is derived from clinical observations and laboratory findings, with no mention of databases, search dates, or eligibility criteria for study inclusion.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that secondary ITP following dengue infection is uncommon but clinically important. It emphasizes that persistent thrombocytopenia after dengue should not be automatically attributed to residual viral effects. The pathogenesis of ITP is described as involving immune dysfunction, including autoantibody-mediated platelet destruction, splenic macrophage phagocytosis, and T-cell-mediated destruction. The case supports an immune-mediated process rather than a direct viral effect.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that a definitive causal relationship between dengue infection and ITP cannot be conclusively established due to the lack of long-term follow-up data. It also highlights the need for further studies to determine the incidence, risk factors, and optimal management strategies for post-dengue ITP.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that clinicians should maintain a high index of suspicion for secondary ITP in patients with persistent thrombocytopenia and bleeding manifestations following dengue infection. It recommends comprehensive evaluation and timely immunosuppressive therapy to reduce morbidity and improve patient outcomes.

莫斯科输入性传染病临床和流行病学结构

Clinical and epidemiological structure of imported infectious diseases in Moscow
作者： E. Yu. Pylaeva, Н. Н. Зверева, А. A. Erovichenkov, Ruslan Faridovich Sayfullin, Шамшева Ольга Васильевна, Natalia Yu. Pshenichcnaya, I. N. Tyurin
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究调查了国际旅行后返回莫斯科的儿童和成人中输入性传染病(包括 登革热)的临床和流行病学特征。
证据基础与方法
莫斯科卫生部第一传染病临床医院进行了一项单中心回顾性 队列研究 研究,回顾了 2009 年至 2019 年间在国际旅行期间或之后患病并入住该医院隔离病房的 2005 名成人和 501 名儿童的病历。使用 IBM SPSS Statistics 26.0 版进行统计分析。研究参与者的年龄从 1 个月到 86 岁不等。
共识与结论
在儿童中,登革热 是最常被诊断出的 媒介 感染,占病例的 88.9 %。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据并未报告有关登革热的任何争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
所提供的证据并未报告对登革热的具体研究或实践影响。

Clinical and epidemiological structure of imported infectious diseases in Moscow

Authors: E. Yu. Pylaeva, Н. Н. Зверева, А. A. Erovichenkov, Ruslan Faridovich Sayfullin, Шамшева Ольга Васильевна, Natalia Yu. Pshenichcnaya, I. N. Tyurin
Original abstract (English)
The expansion of international tourism and increasing migration have led to a higher risk of the emergence and spread of various infectious diseases, including those that are not endemic to Russia. Therefore, studying the characteristics of infectious diseases in children and adults returning from international travel is of considerable importance. To analyze the clinical and epidemiological characteristics of imported infectious diseases in children and adults after international travel. Materials and Methods . We conducted a single-center retrospective cohort study at Infectious Clinical Hospital No. 1 of the Moscow Department of Health. Medical records of 2005 adults and 501 children who developed illness during or after international travel and were admitted to the hospital’s isolation ward after returning from abroad between 2009 and 2019 were retrospectively reviewed. Statistical analysis was performed using IBM SPSS Statistics version 26.0. The age of the study participants ranged from 1 month to 86 years. Among hospitalized patients, children 1–3 years accounted for the largest proportion (41.9 %). Children were hospitalized more frequently during the summer (176 cases, 35.1 %; p<0.001) and autumn (139 (27,7; 23,6-31,6), p<0,05), whereas adults were more commonly hospitalized in the winter (496 cases, 24.7 %; p<0.001) and in the spring (526 (26,2; 24,5–28,1), p<0,05). In the epidemiological history, travel to the Republic of Türkiye was most common among children (17.0 %), while travel to the Kingdom of Thailand predominated among adults (24.9 %). Compared with adults, children were more frequently diagnosed with enterovirus infection (37.0 % vs 19.2 %, p<0.001) among infections with a fecal-oral transmission route, and pertussis (1.5 % vs 0.1 %, p<0.05) among infections with an airborne transmission route. Among children, dengue fever was the most frequently diagnosed vector-borne infection (88.9 %). We identified the epidemiological characteristics and etiological structure of imported infectious diseases and demonstrated seasonal, geographic, and nosological differences in infectious diseases among children compared with adults after international travel.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study investigates the clinical and epidemiological characteristics of imported infectious diseases, including dengue fever, among children and adults returning to Moscow after international travel.
Evidence base and method
A single-center retrospective cohort study was conducted at Infectious Clinical Hospital No. 1 of the Moscow Department of Health, reviewing medical records of 2005 adults and 501 children who developed illness during or after international travel and were admitted to the hospital’s isolation ward between 2009 and 2019. Statistical analysis was performed using IBM SPSS Statistics version 26.0. The age of the study participants ranged from 1 month to 86 years.
Consensus and conclusions
Among children, dengue fever was the most frequently diagnosed vector-borne infection, accounting for 88.9 % of cases.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report any controversies or evidence gaps regarding dengue.
Research and practice implications
The supplied evidence does not report specific research or practice implications for dengue.

Epidemic and emerging disease alerts in the Pacific as of 06 October 2026 - ReliefWeb

Epidemic and emerging disease alerts in the Pacific as of 06 October 2026 - ReliefWeb
新闻简报ReliefWeb 2026 年 10 月 6 日的报告强调了太平洋地区正在发生的登革热 暴发,特别是在马绍尔群岛、萨摩亚、瓦努阿图以及瓦利斯和富图纳群岛。在马绍尔群岛,2026年8月15日至9月25日期间,第10次暴发情况报告记录了112例确诊病例,无死亡病例,7例住院治疗。马朱罗的确诊病例发病率为每1,000人4.2例,埃贝耶为每1,000人1.5例,其中DENV-1是唯一检测到的血清型。在萨摩亚,第 39 周报告新增 35 例病例,较前一周增加 169%,其中有 5 例实验室确诊病例。瓦努阿图 暴发 共有 169 例确诊病例,其中 16 例住院治疗,无死亡病例,登革热红色警报仍然有效。瓦利斯和富图纳群岛报告了 95 例确诊或疑似病例,红色警报也已生效。该报告还指出了澳大利亚的麻疹 暴发 和瓦努阿图的百日咳 暴发,但这不是主要焦点。应对措施包括持续调查、接触者追踪和社区宣传工作,当前数据中没有 重症登革热 或死亡报告。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该报告于2026年10月6日发布。事件日期为2026年8月15日至2026年9月27日,截至2026年10月1日报告的持续病例。
地点与对象
The outbreak is reported in the Marshall Islands (Majuro and Ebeye), Samoa, Vanuatu (Efate, Santo, Tafea Province, and West Tanna Area Council), and Wallis and Futuna. The affected population includes children under 15 years, who account for 73 percent of cases, and the general population in these regions.
事件
马绍尔群岛、萨摩亚、瓦努阿图、瓦利斯和富图纳群岛报告了登革热 暴发。暴发 已确诊,有实验室确诊病例并正在进行监测。该报告还提到了澳大利亚的麻疹 暴发 和瓦努阿图的百日咳 暴发,但这些并不是分析的主要重点。
规模、影响与风险
马绍尔群岛报告了 112 例确诊病例,无死亡病例,7 人住院治疗。萨摩亚报告新增病例 35 例,较前一周增加 169%。瓦努阿图报告了 169 例确诊病例,其中 16 例住院治疗,无死亡病例。瓦利斯和富图纳群岛有 95 例确诊或疑似病例。DENV-1 是唯一检测到的血清型,登革热红色警报仍然有效。
应对、状态与不确定性
瓦努阿图的 暴发 包括正在进行的调查、接触者追踪和社区宣传工作。马绍尔群岛、萨摩亚、瓦努阿图、瓦利斯和富图纳群岛仍保持登革热红色警报。响应状态正在调查中,尚未报告确诊死亡或 重症登革热 病例。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Epidemic and emerging disease alerts in the Pacific as of 06 October 2026 - ReliefWeb

News Brief
The ReliefWeb report from 6 October 2026 highlights ongoing dengue outbreaks in the Pacific region, specifically in the Marshall Islands, Samoa, Vanuatu, and Wallis and Futuna. In the Marshall Islands, the 10th Outbreak Situation Report records 112 confirmed cases, with no deaths and seven hospitalisations, from 15 August to 25 September 2026. The confirmed-case attack rate is 4.2 per 1,000 in Majuro and 1.5 per 1,000 in Ebeye, with DENV-1 being the only serotype detected. In Samoa, 35 new cases were reported in EpiWeek 39, a 169 percent increase from the previous week, with five laboratory-confirmed cases. Vanuatu's outbreak includes 169 confirmed cases, with 16 hospitalisations and no deaths, and the red alert for dengue remains active. Wallis and Futuna reported 95 confirmed or probable cases, with the red alert also in effect. The report also notes a measles outbreak in Australia and a pertussis outbreak in Vanuatu, but these are not the primary focus. The response includes ongoing investigation, contact tracing, and community awareness efforts, with no severe dengue or deaths reported in the current data.
